US$1,773.8 million in 2024, benefiting from our strong points-   由於我們強勁的終端銷售（「終端銷售」）表現及去

and effective promotional activities further accelerated end     求。與2023年相比，我們於報告期內實現純利增長
consumer demand. We achieved a 403.4% growth in net              403.4%，收入規模有所恢復，效率有所提高。
profit during the Reporting Period as compared to 2023 with

 MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
 管理層討論與分析

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                     股息政策
Pursuant to the dividend policy adopted by the Company (the         根據本公司採納的股息政策（「股息政策」），股息宣
“Dividend Policy”), the declaration of dividends is subject to      派由董事會酌情釐定及（如需要）須經股東批准。董
the discretion of the Board, and, if necessary, the approval of     事會考慮宣派及派發股息時，須考慮本集團的經營
the Shareholders. In considering the declaration and payment        業績、現金流量、財務狀況、本公司派發股息的法
of dividends, the Board shall take into account the Group’s         定及監管限制、本公司的資本需求、未來業務計劃
results of operations, cash flows, financial condition, statutory   及前景以及董事會可能認為相關的任何其他因素。
and regulatory restrictions on the payment of dividends by          任何股息的宣派及派發以及金額亦將須遵守本公司
the Company, the Company’s capital requirements, future             組織章程細則（「組織章程細則」）及所有適用的法律
business plans and prospects and any other factors that the         及法規。董事可不時重新評估股息政策。有關股息
Board may consider relevant. Any declaration and payment,           政策的進一步詳情載於本年報第41 頁的企業管治報
as well as the amount, of any dividend will also be subject         告。
to the articles of association of the Company (the “Articles
of Association”) and all applicable laws and regulations.
The Directors may reassess the Dividend Policy from time to
time. Further details of the Dividend Policy are provided in
the Corporate Governance Report on page 41 of this Annual
Report.

and chief executive officer roles, and the responsibilities of      行政總裁的職責均由潘龍泉先生承擔。董事會相

Longquan. The Board believes that vesting the responsibilities      本集團內部領導貫徹一致，使本集團的整體策略規
of both chairman and chief executive officer in the same            劃更有效及更具效率。此外，董事會合共七名董事

within the Group and enables more effective and efficient           立意見，以保障本公司及股東的整體利益。
overall strategic planning of the Group. Besides, with three

Revenue recognition
收入確認

The Key Audit Matter                                       How the matter was addressed in our audit
關鍵審核事項                                                     我們於審核過程中處理有關事項的方式


The Group’s revenue principally comprises sales of           Our audit procedures to assess the recognition of
power tools, outdoor power equipment and related             revenue included the following:
products.

The directors are responsible for the other information. The       董事對其他資料負責。其他資料包括年報所載的所
other information comprises all the information included in the    有資料，惟不包括綜合財務報表及我們就此所作的
annual report, other than the consolidated financial statements    核數師報告。
and our auditor’s report thereon.


Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not      我們有關綜合財務報表的意見並無涵蓋其他資料，
cover the other information and we do not express any form of      我們亦並無就此發表任何形式的核證結論。
assurance conclusion thereon.


In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial         就我們對綜合財務報表的審核而言，我們的責任是
statements, our responsibility is to read the other information    閱讀其他資料，從而考慮其他資料是否與綜合財務
and, in doing so, consider whether the other information           報表或我們在審核過程中獲悉的資料存在重大不
is materially inconsistent with the consolidated financial         符，或似乎存在重大錯誤陳述。
statements or our knowledge obtained in the audit or otherwise
appears to be materially misstated.


If, based on the work we have performed, we conclude that          倘若我們基於已完成的工作認為其他資料出現重大
there is a material misstatement of this other information, we     錯誤陳述，我們須報告該事實。我們就此並無須報
are required to report that fact. We have nothing to report in     告事項。
this regard.

1     GENERAL INFORMATION                                            1      一般資料
      Chervon Holdings Limited (the “Company”) was                          泉峰控股有限公司（「本公司」）為於1999年2
      incorporated in Hong Kong on February 19, 1999 as                     月19日於香港註冊成立的有限公司，註冊辦
      a limited liability company with its registered office at             事處位於香港九龍荔枝角長順街 7 號西頓中
      Unit 04, 22/F, Saxon Tower, 7 Cheung Shun Street, Lai                 心22樓04室。本公司股份於2021年12月30
      Chi Kok, Kowloon, Hong Kong. The Company’s shares                     日於香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市。
      were listed on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange of
      Hong Kong Limited on December 30, 2021.


      The Company is an investment holding company. The                     本公司為投資控股公司。本公司及其附屬公
      Company and its subsidiaries (together, “the Group”)                  司（統稱「本集團」）主要從事電動工具、戶外
      are principally engaged in researching, developing,                   動力設備及相關產品的研究、開發、製造、
      manufacturing, testing, sales, and after-sale services                測試、銷售及售後服務。主要附屬公司的資
      for power tools, outdoor power equipment and related                  料載於附註16。
      products. The information of the principal subsidiaries
      is set out in Note 16.

      Standards (HKASs) and Interpretations issued by the                   以及香港公司條例的規定。該等財務報表亦
      Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants                   遵守香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則
      (HKICPA), and the requirements of the Hong Kong                       的適用披露條文。本集團採納的重大會計政

 2        MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                  2     重大會計政策（續）
          (CONTINUED)
 (b)      Basis of preparation of the financial statements              (b)   財務報表編製基準

          The consolidated financial statements of the Group                  本集團截至2024 年12 月31 日止年度的綜合
          for the year ended December 31, 2024 comprise the                   財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司以及本集
          Company and its subsidiaries and the Group’s interest               團於聯營公司的權益。
          in an associate.


          The measurement basis used in the preparation of the                編製綜合財務報表時使用的計量基準為歷史
          consolidated financial statements is the historical cost            成本法，但以下資產及負債按下文會計政策
          basis except that the following assets and liabilities are          中解釋的公允價值列示。
          stated at their fair value as explained in the accounting
          policies as set out below.


          The preparation of financial statements in conformity               編製符合香港財務報告準則的財務報表要求
          with HKFRSs requires management to make                             管理層須作出判斷、估計及假設，而該等判
          judgements, estimates and assumptions that affect                   斷、估計及假設會影響政策應用以及所呈報
          the application of policies and reported amounts of                 的資產、負債、收入及開支金額。該等估計
          assets, liabilities, income and expenses. The estimates             及相關假設乃基於過往經驗及在有關情況下
          and associated assumptions are based on historical                  被認為屬合理的各項其他因素，而所得結果
          experience and various other factors that are believed              乃用作判斷顯然無法輕易通過其他來源獲得
          to be reasonable under the circumstances, the results               有關資產與負債賬面值的依據。實際結果或
          of which form the basis of making the judgements                    有別於該等估計。
          about carrying values of assets and liabilities that are
          not readily apparent from other sources. Actual results
          may differ from these estimates.


          The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed               該等估計及相關假設被持續審閱。若會計估
          on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates              計的修訂僅影響作出修訂的期間，將會於該
          are recognized in the period in which the estimate is               期間確認，若修訂影響當期及未來期間，則
          revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the         會於作出修訂及其後期間確認。
          period of the revision and future periods if the revision
          affects both current and future periods.

          Measurement of ECLs                                                   計量預期信貸虧損

          losses. Generally, credit losses are measured as the                  損。信貸虧損通常按合約及預期金額之間的
          present value of all expected cash shortfalls between                 所有預期現金差額的現值計量。
          the contractual and expected amounts.

          following rates if the effect is material:                            下貼現率貼現：

                    receivables: effective interest rate determined at                款項：初步確認時釐定的實際利率或
                    initial recognition or an approximation thereof;                  其近似值；及
                    and


          –         variable-rate financial assets: current effective           －     浮動利率金融資產：當前的實際利
                    interest rate.                                                    率。

      credit risk when its credit risk rating is equivalent to the          的「投資級別」定義時，本集團認為該金融工
      globally understood definition of ‘investment grade’.                 具的信貸風險較低。


      ECLs are remeasured at each reporting date to reflect                 預期信貸虧損於各報告日期重新計量，以反
      changes in the financial instrument’s credit risk since               映金融工具自初始確認以來的信貸風險變
      initial recognition. Any change in the ECL amount is                  動。預期信貸虧損金額的任何變動均於損益
      recognized as an impairment gain or loss in profit or                 中確認為減值收益或虧損。本集團就所有金
      loss. The Group recognizes an impairment gain or                      融工具確認減值收益或虧損，並透過虧損撥
      loss for all financial instruments with a corresponding               備賬對其賬面值作出相應調整，惟按公允價
      adjustment to their carrying amount through a loss                    值計量且其變動計入其他全面收益（可撥回）
      allowance account, except for investments in non-equity               計量的非股本證券投資除外，其虧損撥備於
      securities that are measured at FVOCI (recycling), for                其他全面收益確認，並於公允價值儲備（可
      which the loss allowance is recognized in OCI and                     撥回）累計，不會減少財務狀況表中金融資

          Credit-impaired financial assets                                    信貸減值金融資產


          At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether a                於各報告日期，本集團評估金融資產有否出
          financial asset is credit-impaired. A financial asset is            現信貸減值。當發生一項或多項對金融資產
          credit-impaired when one or more events that have a                 估計未來現金流量產生不利影響的事件時，
          detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows               金融資產即被視為出現信貸減值。
          of the financial asset have occurred.


          Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired                  金融資產出現信貸減值的跡象包括以下可觀
          includes the following observable events:                           察事件：


          –         significant financial difficulties of the debtor;         －   債務人面臨重大財務困難；


          –         a breach of contract, such as a default or                －   違反合約，如利息或本金付款違約或
                    delinquency in interest or principal payments;                拖欠；


          –         the restructuring of a loan or advance by the             －   本集團根據其他情況下不會考慮的條
                    Group on terms that the Group would not                       款重組貸款或墊款；
                    consider otherwise;


          –         it is probable that the debtor will enter into            －   債務人有可能破產或需要進行其他財
                    bankruptcy or other financial reorganization; or              務重組；或


          –         the disappearance of an active market for a               －   發行人陷入財務困難導致證券失去活
                    security because of financial difficulties of the             躍市場。
                    issuer.


          Write-off policy                                                    撇銷政策


          The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written           倘日後回收不可實現，金融資產的賬面總值
          off to the extent that there is no realistic prospect of            將撇銷。該情況通常出現在本集團以其他方
          recovery. This is generally the case when the Group                 式釐定債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生足
          otherwise determines that the debtor does not have                  夠現金流量以償還撇銷金額時。
          assets or sources of income that could generate
          sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject to
          the write-off.


          Subsequent recoveries of an asset that was previously               過往撇銷資產的後續回收於回收期間於損益
          written off are recognized as a reversal of impairment in           確認為減值撥回。
          profit or loss in the period in which the recovery occurs.

(ii)   Impairment of other non-current assets                         (ii)    其他非流動資產減值

       At each reporting date, the Group reviews the                          於各報告日期，本集團審閱其非金融資產
       carrying amounts of its non-financial assets (other                   （存貨及遞延稅項資產除外）的賬面值，以釐
       than inventories and deferred tax assets) to determine                 定是否有任何減值跡象。倘存在任何有關跡
       whether there is any indication of impairment. If any                  象，則會估計資產的可收回金額。每年對商
       such indication exists, then the asset’s recoverable                   譽進行減值測試。
       amount is estimated. Goodwill is tested annually for
       impairment.


       For impairment testing, assets are grouped together                    就減值測試而言，資產納入持續使用產生現
       into the smallest group of assets that generates cash                  金流入的最小組別，該組別基本獨立於其他
       inflows from continuing use that are largely independent               資產或現金產生單位（「現金產生單位」）的現
       of the cash inflows of other assets or cash-generating                 金流入。業務合併產生的商譽分配至預期受
       units (“CGU”s). Goodwill arising from a business                       益於合併協同效應的現金產生單位或現金產
       combination is allocated to CGUs or groups of CGUs                     生單位組別。
       that are expected to benefit from the synergies of the
       combination.


       The recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is the                       資產或現金產生單位的可收回金額為其使用
       greater of its value in use and its fair value less costs of           價值與其公允價值減出售成本兩者中的較高
       disposal. Value in use is based on the estimated future                者。使用價值乃基於估計未來現金流量，使
       cash flows, discounted to their present value using                    用能反映現時市場對金錢的時間價值的評估
       a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market                   及該資產或現金產生單位的特有風險的除稅
       assessments of the time value of money and the risks                   前折現率折減至其現值。
       specific to the asset or CGU.


       An impairment loss is recognized if the carrying amount                倘資產或現金產生單位的賬面值超過其可收
       of an asset or CGU exceeds its recoverable amount.                     回金額，則確認減值虧損。


       Impairment losses are recognized in profit or loss. They               減值虧損於損益內確認。減值虧損首先用以
       are allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any               調低分攤至現金產生單位的任何商譽的賬面
       goodwill allocated to the CGU, and then to reduce the                  值，然後按比例調低現金產生單位內其他資
       carrying amounts of the other assets in the CGU on a                   產的賬面值。
       pro rata basis.


       An impairment loss in respect of goodwill is not                       有關商譽之減值虧損將不予撥回。就其他資
       reversed. For other assets, an impairment loss is                      產而言，僅當所產生的賬面值不超過假設並
       reversed only to the extent that the resulting carrying                無確認減值虧損而應釐定的賬面值（已扣除
       amount does not exceed the carrying amount that                        折舊或攤銷）方可撥回減值虧損。
       would have been determined, net of depreciation
       or amortization, if no impairment loss had been
       recognized.

 (m)      Inventories                                                  (m)     存貨

          formula and comprises all costs of purchase, costs of                成本、加工成本及將存貨運至現時地點及達

          In the case of work in progress, costs include direct                （根據正常運營能力計算）。
          labour and appropriate share of overheads based on
          normal operating capacity.


          Net realizable value is the estimated selling price in the           可變現淨值按日常業務過程中的估計售價減
          ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of              完成生產及銷售所需估計成本計算。
          completion and the estimated costs necessary to make
          the sale.


          Net realizable value represents the estimated selling                可變現淨值指估計售價減任何完成生產所需
          price less any estimated costs of completion and costs               估計成本及出售物業將產生的成本。
          to be incurred in selling the property.

(u)   Income tax                                                      (u)    所得稅

      Income tax expense comprises current tax and deferred                  所得稅開支包括當期稅項以及遞延稅項。其
      tax. It is recognized in profit or loss except to the                  於損益確認，惟與業務合併或直接於權益或

      and any adjustments to the tax payable or receivable                   收稅項作出的任何調整。當期應付或應收稅
      in respect of previous years. The amount of current tax                項金額為預期支付或收取的稅項金額的最佳
      payable or receivable is the best estimate of the tax                  估計，反映與所得稅有關的任何不確定性。
      amount expected to be paid or received that reflects                   其採用於報告日期已頒佈或實質上已頒佈的
      any uncertainty related to income taxes. It is measured                稅率計量。當期稅項亦包括股息產生的任何
      using tax rates enacted or substantively enacted at the                稅項。
      reporting date. Current tax also includes any tax arising
      from dividends.

(v)   Provisions and contingent liabilities                         (v)    撥備及或有負債

      Generally provisions are determined by discounting the               撥備通常通過按反映當時市場對貨幣時間價
      expected future cash flows at a pre-tax rate that reflects           值及負債特定風險的評估的除稅前利率貼現
      current market assessment of the time value of money                 預期未來現金流量而釐定。
      and the risks specific to the liability.


      A provision for warranties is recognized when the                    保修撥備乃於相關產品或服務售出時根據過
      underlying products or services are sold, based on                   往保修數據及權衡保修可能出現的結果及有
      historical warranty data and a weighting of possible                 關的可能性確認。
      outcomes against their associated probabilities.


      A provision for onerous contracts is measured at the                 多項虧損性合約的撥備乃按終止合約預期成
      present value of the lower of the expected cost of                   本及繼續合約預期成本淨額（以較低者為準）
      terminating the contract and the expected net cost of                的現值計量，而繼續合約預期成本淨額乃根
      continuing with the contract, which is determined based              據履行該合約項下責任的增量成本及與履行
      on the incremental costs of fulfilling the obligation under          該合約直接相關的其他成本的分配釐定。於
      that contract and an allocation of other costs directly              計提撥備前，本集團就該合約相關資產確認
      related to fulfilling that contract. Before a provision is           任何減值虧損（請參閱附註2(l)(ii)）。
      established, the Group recognizes any impairment loss
      on the assets associated with that contract (see Note 2
      (l)(ii)).


      A restructuring provision is recognised only when the                重組撥備於符合撥備的一般確認標準時方獲
      general recognition criteria for provision are met. The              確認。重組責任往往具有建設性。建設性重
      obligation for a restructuring is often constructive. A              組責任於出現下列情況時方會產生：詳盡的
      constructive restructuring obligation arises only when               正式計劃識別出主要重組特性；及對該等受
      there is a detailed formal plan identifying the main                 影響者合理預期實體將開始執行計劃或向該
      features of the restructuring; and a valid expectation               等受影響者公佈主要特性以進行重組。
      in those affected that the entity will carry out the
      restructuring by starting to implement the plan or
      announcing its main features to those affected.


      Restructuring provision includes only the direct                     重組撥備僅包括源自重組的直接開支，該項
      expenditures arising from the restructuring, which are               開支屬重組的必要開支，而非有關實體持續
      necessarily entailed by the restructuring and not those              活動的開支。於計量重組撥備時概無計及任
      associated with the entity’s ongoing activities. Any                 何資產銷售的預期收益。
      expected gains on the sale of assets are not considered
      in measuring a restructuring provision.

       (b)   Interest income                                                (b)       利息收益

             Interest income is recognized using the effective                        利息收益按實際利率法確認。「實際

             of the financial asset. In calculating interest

             income, the effective interest rate is applied to

             the gross carrying amount of the asset (when

(c)   Pillar Two income taxes                                       (c)    支柱二所得稅

      In 2021, the Organisation for Economic Co-operation                  於2021年，經濟合作及發展組織就適用於大
      and Development published the Global Anti-Base                       型跨國企業的新全球最低稅制改革發佈全球
      Erosion Model Rules (“Pillar Two model rules”) for a                 反基礎侵蝕模型規則（「支柱二示範規則」）。
      new global minimum tax reform applicable to large                    本集團運營所在若干司法管轄區已在該框架
      multinational enterprises. Certain jurisdictions in which            基礎上實施支柱二所得稅立法，該等支柱二
      the Group operates have implemented Pillar Two                       所得稅法律於2024年1月1日生效。
      income tax legislation based on this framework, and
      those Pillar Two income tax laws became effective on
      January 1, 2024.


      The Pillar Two income taxes are levied on subsidiaries               支柱二所得稅乃根據越南、加拿大、英國及
      under the new tax laws in Vietnam, Canada, United                    若干歐洲國家的新稅法對附屬公司徵稅，該
      Kingdom and certain European countries which                         等國家出台了國內最低補充稅，自2024 年1
      introduced a domestic minimum top-up tax effective                   月 1 日起生效。截至 2024 年 12 月 31 日止年
      from January 1, 2024. The Group didn’t recognise any                 度，本集團並無確認與支柱二所得稅有關的
      current tax expense related to Pillar Two income taxes               任何當期稅項，原因是本集團評估認為支柱
      for the year ended December 31, 2024, as the Group                   二示範規則對當期稅項開支並無重大影響。
      assessed Pillar Two model rules had no material effect
      on the current tax expense.


      The Group has applied the temporary mandatory                        本集團已應用暫時強制豁免，以確認及披露
      exception to recognising and disclosing information                  有關支柱二所得稅的遞延稅項資產及負債的
      about deferred tax assets and liabilities related to Pillar          資料，並將於產生時將稅項入賬列作當期稅
      Two income taxes and accounted for the tax as current                項。
      tax when incurred.


      Other jurisdictions in which the Group operates are in               本集團運營所在其他司法管轄區正在實施其
      the process of implementing their Pillar Two income                  支柱二所得稅立法。因此，本集團於該等司
      tax legislation. Therefore, it is possible that the Group            法管轄區可能須繳納額外的支柱二所得稅。
      may be subject to additional Pillar Two income taxes in
      those jurisdictions.

          rate in the five-year projection period. The discount               反映通常股權投資增加的風險以及特定現金

 (e)      Capital management                                            (e)   資本管理

          The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital                本集團管理資本的首要目標乃保障本集團能
          are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a               夠持續經營，以便透過對與風險水平相應的
          going concern, so that it can continue to provide returns           產品及服務定價以及獲得合理成本的融資，
          for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,               繼續為股東創造回報及為其他持份者帶來利
          by pricing products and services commensurately with                益。
          the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
          reasonable cost.


          The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages                本集團定期積極對資本架構進行檢討及管
          its capital structure to maintaining a balance between              理，以在較高股東回報情況下可能伴隨的較
          the higher shareholders returns that might be possible              高借貸水平與良好資本狀況帶來的好處及保
          with higher levels of borrowings and the advantages                 障之間取得平衡，並根據經濟狀況的變化對
          and security afforded by a sound capital position, and              資本結構作出調整。
          makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
          changes in economic conditions.


          The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis of            本集團利用淨資本負債比率監察其資本結
          a net gearing ratio. For this purpose, the Group defines            構。就此而言，本集團將淨負債界定為流動
          net debt as total current and non-current bank loans                及非流動銀行貸款及租賃負債總額減現金及
          and lease liabilities less cash and cash equivalents                現金等價物及已抵押存款。本集團將資本界
          and pledged deposits. The Group defines capital as                  定為包括所有權益組成部分。
          including all components of equity.

          Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency           本集團業務面臨正常業務過程中產生的信
          risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s                    貸、流動性、利率及貨幣風險。
          business.


          The Group’s exposure to these risks and financial risk              本集團面臨的該等風險及本集團為管理該等
          management policies and practices used by the Group                 風險而採用的財務風險管理政策及慣例描述
          to manage these risks are described below:                          如下：


 (a)      Credit risk                                                   (a)   信貸風險

          Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will             信貸風險指對手方將違反合約義務而導致本
          default on its contractual obligations resulting in a               集團產生財務虧損的風險。本集團信貸風險
          financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is             主要歸因於貿易及其他應收款項。本集團面
          primarily attributable to trade and other receivables.              對來自現金及現金等價物、已抵押存款及應
          The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash               收票據的信貸風險有限，原因是對手方為信
          and cash equivalents, pledged deposits and bills                    譽狀況良好的知名金融機構，本集團認為其
          receivable is limited because the counterparties are                信貸風險較低。
          reputable financial institutions with high credit standing,
          for which the Group considers to have low credit risk.


          Except for the financial guarantees given by the Group              除附註36所載由本集團提供的財務擔保外，
          as set out in Note 36, the Group does not provide any               本集團並無任何其他將令本集團承受信貸風
          other guarantees which would expose the Group to                    險的擔保。於報告期末有關該等財務擔保的
          credit risk. The maximum exposure to credit risk in                 最高信貸風險敞口乃披露於附註36。
          respect of these financial guarantees at the end of the
          reporting period is disclosed in Note 36.


          Trade receivables                                                   貿易應收款項

          The Group has established a credit risk management                  本集團已制定信貸風險管理政策，據此對所
          policy under which individual credit evaluations are                有需要超過一定金額的信貸的客戶均進行個
          performed on all customers requiring credit over                    別信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往到期
          a certain amount. These evaluations focus on the                    作出付款的記錄及目前的支付能力，並計及
          customer’s past history of making payments when                     客戶的特定資料以及有關客戶經營所在經濟
          due and current ability to pay and take into account                環境的資料。貿易應收款項自賬單日期起計
          information specific to the customer as well as                     30 至180 日內到期。正常情況下，本集團不
          pertaining to the economic environment in which the                 會收取客戶的抵押品。
          customer operates. Trade receivables are due within
          30 to 180 days from the date of billing. Normally, the
          Group does not obtain collateral from customers.

(b)   Liquidity risk                                               (b)     流動性風險

      Individual operating entities within the Group are                   本集團旗下個別經營實體負責各自現金管
      responsible for their own cash management, including                 理，包括現金盈餘短期投資及為滿足預期現
      the short-term investment of cash surpluses and the                  金要求而籌集貸款，惟借款超出一定授權預
      raising of loans to cover expected cash demands,                     定水平後須取得母公司董事會批准。本集團
      subject to approval by the parent company’s board                    的政策為定期監察其流動資金需求及其遵守
      when the borrowings exceed certain predetermined                     借貸契諾的情況，以確保維持充足的現金儲
      levels of authority. The Group’s policy is to regularly              備及來自主要金融機構的足夠承諾額度以滿
      monitor its liquidity requirements and its compliance                足其短期及長期的流動資金需求。
      with lending covenants to ensure that it maintains
      sufficient reserves of cash and adequate committed
      lines of funding from major financial institutions to meet
      its liquidity requirements in the short and longer term.
